{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["The cycle for the air engine proposed by Joule. In it air is taken by a pump from a cold chamber and compressed adiabatically until its pressure is equal to that of the air in a hot chamber, into which it is then delivered, thereby displacing an equal amount of hot air into the engine cylinder. Here it expands adiabatically to the temperature of the cold chamber into which it is finally exhausted. This cycle, reversed, is used in refrigeration."],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics","thermodynamics"]}],"upstream_line":5265489,"upstream_offset":12277151857,"upstream_record_sha256":"13b4daf4e55f745d02aac78c1982a7b7a6115040d681bab3011c3e8606edebde","word":"Joule's cycle"}

{"forms":[{"form":"bullet dwarfs","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"bullet dwarf galaxy","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An ultradiffuse galaxy and dwarf galaxy with little or no dark matter, but with young very luminous globular clusters, about the same age as most of the stars in the galaxy.","An ultradiffuse dwarf galaxy that resulted from the collisions of two dwarf galaxies, where the stars, gas, and, dust, collide, bind, and shocked together, creating a new single dwarf galaxy and starburst, while the companion dark matter haloes continue onward on their former paths, separating from the now singular galaxy."],"topics":["astronomy","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["An ultradiffuse galaxy and dwarf galaxy with little or no dark matter, but with young very luminous globular clusters, about the same age as most of the stars in the galaxy.","Any of a series of ultradiffuse dwarfs lined up on the path of the two dark matter haloes after collision, the remnants of the collision of two, perhaps not dwarf, galaxies, falling behind due to matter shockwave interactions the dark matter did not experience."],"topics":["astronomy","natural-sciences"]}],"upstream_line":10658269,"upstream_offset":25736468001,"upstream_record_sha256":"3a68a13daea1ae5145647e33f3cf0c3f9f01643ab71502e48fdad63f8d69c182","word":"bullet dwarf"}

It may sometimes include slaty structure or cleavage, though the latter is usually independent of any mineral constituent, and transverse to the bedding, it having been produced by pressure."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A set of submanifolds of a given manifold, each of which is of lower dimension than it, but which, taken together, are coextensive with it."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences","topology"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/fəʊlɪˈeɪʃn/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"process of forming pages","word":"pagination"},{"sense":"growth and arrangement of leaves","word":"vernation"}],"upstream_line":236646,"upstream_offset":1163839967,"upstream_record_sha256":"f572d434625c993b5286dbdf7584e3f7f7a5ae9abe158f6fe6bc3d743075206d","word":"foliation"}

{"forms":[{"form":"free Boolean algebras","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A field of sets whose elements are equivalent to Boolean formulas (or, perhaps more precisely, equivalence classes of Boolean formulas). Starting with a set of n variables which are independent of each other and are called generators, the power set of this set has 2ⁿmembers which may be called atoms and are valuations of the n variables: a valuation can be considered to be a set of variables which are \"true\" under that valuation, or a conjunction of generators (such that variables not included in that set are included in negated form in the equivalent conjunction). Then the power set of the set of atoms yields a set of 2^(2ⁿ) members which are the elements of the said field of sets. These elements correspond to Boolean formulas: a formula can be considered to be a set of valuations which make the formula true, or a linear combination (i.e., a disjunction) of atoms."],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":6985053,"upstream_offset":15504783165,"upstream_record_sha256":"32a720e5af0cc05e8f62460759407ec1cc0393f3cf9c5eb191dee44af7346179","word":"free Boolean algebra"}
